TL;DR: 本文探讨了超轻暗物质作为轴向矢量场的可能性，分析了其与费米子的耦合机制及对实验探测的影响，并指出其与弦理论中Kalb-Ramond暗物质的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超轻暗物质的新候选者——轴向矢量场，并为其设计新型实验探测方案。

Method: 理论分析轴向矢量场与费米子（通过轴向矢量流或暗电偶极矩）及光子（需引入光子质量）的耦合机制，结合对偶性讨论其与两形式场（Kalb-Ramond场）的联系。

Result: 轴向矢量暗物质在自旋力矩上强于轴子暗物质；其‘轴向-光子’耦合下背景磁场效应被抑制，最强探针为偏振测量、超导腔内电场及宇宙微波背景辐射。

Conclusion: 轴向矢量是可行且具实验可探测性的超轻暗物质模型，其物理现象和探测策略有别于传统轴子模型，并自然关联到弦理论中的Kalb-Ramond场。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter could be an axial vector field, a simple possibility which motivates new experiments. Couplings to fermions, through an axial vector current or a dark electric dipole moment operator, lead to enhanced spin torques compared to axion dark matter. An axial vector also has an analogue of the axion-photon coupling, though its consistent realization requires a photon mass. Under this "axial-photon" coupling, the effect of a background magnetic field is suppressed, and the strongest experimental probes involve polarimetry, electric fields in superconducting cavities, and the cosmic microwave background. Since a massive axial vector is dual to a massive two-form, our results also apply to "Kalb-Ramond" dark matter motivated by string theory.

</details>


### [104] [On the Numerical Integration of One-Loop Cosmological Collider Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10673)
*Michael Borinsky,Aidan Herderschee,Qianshu Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数值方法，用于计算暴胀宇宙学中一般动量和质量下的标量单圈in-in图对非高斯双谱的贡献，并首次实现了全运动学范围内的单圈双谱直接数值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于最简单的泡图，而对现象学相关质量（特别是非轻质量）下的单圈贡献（如三角图）缺乏探索；但实际中，三角图贡献未必比泡图小，因此需发展适用于一般质量与耦合的数值方法。

Method: 采用Witten-Feynman参数化将dS空间单圈积分转化为由Symanzik图多项式控制的广义Euler-Mellin积分；针对复指数导致的标准数值方法失效问题，提出‘简化Schwinger方法’：解析计算高度振荡子积分，得到超几何函数核，剩余积分用标准数值求积完成。

Result: 成功复现树级双谱的已知${}_3F_2$解析结果；首次给出泡图与三角图在全运动学范围内的单圈双谱数值结果；据此构建双谱模板并用于CMB数据分析。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了传统数值技术对质量范围的限制，为暴胀模型中高精度非高斯性预测与观测检验提供了普适、可靠的计算工具。

Abstract: In a broad class of inflationary models, the leading non-Gaussian bispectrum arises from one-loop rather than tree-level processes. However, realistic one-loop contributions remain largely unexplored for phenomenologically relevant masses beyond the simplest bubble diagrams. This is insufficient for observational purposes because, for generic masses and couplings, triangle contributions need not be suppressed relative to their bubble counterparts. We present a numerical method for evaluating scalar one-loop in-in diagrams contributing to the inflationary bispectrum at fully general external momenta and masses. First, the Witten-Feynman parameterization reduces de Sitter loop integrals to generalized Euler-Mellin integrals governed by Symanzik graph polynomials. Unfortunately, the exponents of the polynomials in the integrand become complex for sufficiently heavy masses, leading to sign problems when evaluating the integral using standard numerical techniques. To remedy this problem, we introduce the reduced Schwinger method, which evaluates a highly oscillatory subintegral analytically to obtain a Gauss hypergeometric kernel, leaving the remaining integrals to standard numerical quadrature. We validate the algorithm by reproducing known analytic results for the tree-level bispectrum in terms of ${}_3F_2$ functions, and then apply it to the one-loop bubble and triangle contributions at general kinematics. This yields the first direct numerical evaluation of the complete one-loop bispectrum, valid across the full kinematic range. Using these numerical results, we construct bispectrum templates and compare them with CMB data.

</details>


### [105] [Re-examining the sensitivity of JWST to decaying axion dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10674)
*Caleb Gemmell,Christopher Dessert,Andrea Caputo,Joshua W. Foster*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过定制的数据处理和非参数推断方法，对eV尺度轴子暗物质的衰变设定了稳健限制，排除了500 meV–2.5 eV质量范围内的QCD轴子作为全部暗物质的可能，并指出当前JWST灵敏度受限于仪器建模与数据处理，难以通过现有数据改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前关于JWST探测eV尺度轴子暗物质的分析结果之间存在数量级分歧、缺乏共识的问题。

Method: 采用定制的数据归约流程和灵活的非参数推断方法，对JWST NIRSpec数据进行分析，并评估不同分析间灵敏度的一致性与可达性。

Result: 排除了质量在500 meV至2.5 eV之间的QCD轴子作为全部暗物质；对100 meV–500 meV的ALP暗物质设定了补充性限制；发现此前最强的声称灵敏度实际上超出了所用数据集所能达到的极限；当前灵敏度受仪器建模与数据处理系统性限制。

Conclusion: 当前JWST对eV尺度轴子暗物质的探测能力已达瓶颈，除非仪器建模与数据归约流程取得显著改进，否则难以进一步提升灵敏度。

Abstract: An eV-scale QCD axion comprising the observed dark matter (DM) abundance is expected to generate a photon line at infrared energies that would be observable or near-observable in data collected by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), as might more general axion-like particles (ALPs) over a broader range of masses and couplings. This has motivated a number of efforts to either forecast JWST sensitivities to a QCD axion or realize them through analyses of publicly available datasets. At present, no consensus exists; leading analyses disagree by as much as an order of magnitude in terms of axion-coupling sensitivity, implying an orders-of-magnitude discrepancy in raw flux density sensitivity, and consistency between the analyses and prior forecasts is unclear. We address these outstanding discrepancies with a bespoke data reduction and flexible nonparametric inference procedure that lead to well-controlled and robust limits on the decay of eV-scale axion DM, consistent with previously forecasted sensitivities. We further demonstrate that the strongest previously claimed sensitivities exceed those attainable by any analysis of the datasets from which they were derived. We exclude QCD axion DM for masses between $500\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $2.5\,\mathrm{eV}$ using NIRSpec data, while setting limits on ALP DM complementary to other astrophysical constraints at masses between $100\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $500\,\mathrm{meV}$. However, we find the sensitivities to be systematically limited, and therefore unlikely to be improved upon by ongoing data collection or re-analysis unless instrumental modeling and data reduction pipelines improve considerably.

</details>


### [106] [Computationally Efficient Description of Medium Response to Jets in Heavy Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10679)
*Jorge Casalderrey-Solana,José Guilherme Milhano,Daniel Pablos,Krishna Rajagopal,Xiaojun Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效计算重离子碰撞中喷注尾迹产生的强子分布的方法（Efficient Wake），利用线性化流体力学的线性特性，结合纵向膨胀和横向径向流效应，并通过模板解与洛伦兹变换实现快速建模；该方法比全非线性(3+1)维流体模拟快数万倍，且精度优于传统Hybrid模型中的简化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有Hybrid模型中对喷注在夸克胶子等离子体中激发的尾迹所导致的强子分布的粗略描述，需兼顾物理准确性与计算效率。

Method: 基于线性化流体力学，在Bjorken流背景下构建模板解；通过旋转与洛伦兹提升将模板映射至含径向流的冻结构形面上，从而纳入横向径向流与纵向膨胀效应。

Result: 与全非线性(3+1)维流体模拟结果吻合良好，显著优于Hybrid模型中原有简化方法；计算速度至少提升数万倍。

Conclusion: Efficient Wake方法在保持高精度的同时极大提升了计算效率，有望在蒙特卡洛模拟（如Hybrid模型）中显著改善对喷注软成分及子结构观测量的实验数据描述。

Abstract: We develop an Efficient Wake procedure for computing the distribution of hadrons originating from jet wakes in heavy ion collisions - the hydrodynamic response of a droplet of quark-gluon plasma to the energy and momentum deposited in it by high-energy partons propagating through it. The procedure employs the linearity of linearized hydrodynamics and takes account of the effects of both longitudinal expansion and transverse radial flow on the hydrodynamic evolution of the wakes and on the resulting particle production at the freezeout hypersurface. It makes repeated use of template solutions to linearized hydrodynamics in a Bjorken flow background with no transverse flow, templates that need only be computed once, and uses suitable rotations and boosts to map fluctuations from these templates to fluctuations at a point on the freezeout hypersurface in a way that incorporates the effects of the radial flow. We benchmark this procedure by comparing its results to results obtained from full $(3+1)$-dimensional nonlinear hydrodynamics calculations, find reasonable agreement, and find that our Efficient Wake procedure yields a much better description of the distribution of hadrons originating from jet wakes than does the older oversimplified procedure employed in the Hybrid Model. And, the Efficient Wake procedure is computationally efficient: it is at least tens of thousands of times faster than full nonlinear hydrodynamics calculations. Hence, we anticipate that when our new procedure is implemented in Monte Carlo analyses of jets in heavy ion collisions, for example in the Hybrid Model, it will greatly improve the description of the soft component of many jet and jet substructure observables as compared to experimental data.

</details>


### [107] [Opening the Topological Portal to Dark Sectors with Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10681)
*Joe Davighi,Admir Greljo,Lia Schöneweiß,Nudzeim Selimovic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了QCD与伪Nambu-Goldstone暗物质之间的拓扑通道现象学，构建了一个弱耦合紫外完备模型，其中矢量中介子规范轻夸克的重子流，并与暗π介子关联；通过LHC、LEP、底偶素及味物理实验检验该模型，并指出Z工厂（如FCC-ee）可在低质量区提供互补探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索QCD与伪Nambu-Goldstone暗物质之间新型拓扑通道的现象学后果，并构建可检验的紫外完备理论框架。

Method: 构建弱耦合紫外完成模型，规范轻夸克的重子流（具体为无轻子组合B₁+B₂−2B₃），连接QCD与SU(2)ₐ/U(1)ₐ暗强子模型；结合热共湮灭计算、共振中介子效应及多类实验约束（LHC双喷注/单喷注、LEP、底偶素、味物理）进行分析；评估FCC-ee等Z工厂在低动量转移下的探测能力。

Result: 成功实现 anomaly-free 紫外完成；给出热暗物质丰度预测并覆盖超出手征微扰论适用范围的参数空间；LHC等现有实验已对部分参数区间构成限制；FCC-ee在未来可显著拓展对长寿命重暗π介子的低质量探测覆盖。

Conclusion: 拓扑通道提供了一种自洽且可检验的暗物质机制，其对称性结构自然导出独特而可观测的高能与低能信号，需结合对撞机与高精度Z工厂实验协同验证。

Abstract: We study the phenomenology of the topological portal between QCD and pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone dark matter proposed in~\cite{Davighi:2024zip}. We construct a weakly coupled ultraviolet completion in which a vector mediator gauges a baryonic current of QCD for the light quark flavours, linking it to dark pions parametrizing the coset $SU(2)_D/U(1)_D$. As a concrete anomaly-free realisation, we gauge the leptophobic combination $B_1+B_2-2B_3$. This completion allows us to calculate thermal coannihilation beyond the regime of validity of the pion effective theory, including resonant mediator effects, and to test the resulting thermal target with boosted-dijet and monojet searches at the LHC, with LEP and bottomonium data, and with flavour observables. These collider signatures follow directly from the symmetry structure required by matching onto the topological portal. At low momentum transfer, the topological operator instead controls the decay of the heavier dark pion, which is long-lived. A future high-statistics $Z$ factory, such as FCC-ee, can probe the low-mass region where hadron-collider sensitivity deteriorates, providing complementary tests of the topological portal.

</details>


### [108] [EFT Approaches to Sommerfeld Enhancement and Bound States in Singular Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10690)
*Arindam Bhattacharya,Rashmish K. Mishra,Tracy R. Slatyer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在奇异相互作用势下暗物质湮灭信号中的Sommerfeld增强（SE）及其与束缚态形成的关系，通过非相对论有效场论（NREFT）的速度幂计数和位置空间短距正则化方法澄清其物理起源，并以赝标量媒介子与狄拉克暗物质的耦合为例，指出导数耦合情形下即使弱耦合也可能出现显著SE，且该SE对紫外（UV）补全敏感，但红外（IR）效应仍可主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异相互作用势下传统薛定谔方程边界条件失效，导致Sommerfeld增强（SE）的计算、物理解释及其与束缚态关系模糊不清，亟需系统性澄清。

Method: 采用非相对论有效场论（NREFT）中的速度幂计数框架，结合位置空间中对奇异势的短距正则化方法，统一处理SE与束缚态计算；以赝标量媒介子与狄拉克暗物质的耦合为具体模型进行分析。

Result: 在导数耦合赝标量模型中，当有效理论截断能标远大于暗物质质量时，即使弱耦合也可产生显著Sommerfeld增强及束缚态；该SE依赖于短距紫外补全，但红外动力学可独立贡献非可忽略的SE。

Conclusion: Sommerfeld增强在奇异势下的起源可通过NREFT与正则化方法清晰理解；其存在与否及大小不仅取决于耦合强度，更关键地受紫外-红外物理协同影响，尤其在导数耦合有效理论中不可忽略。

Abstract: The Sommerfeld enhancement (SE) from long-range interactions, and the related bound state formation rate, can be important non-perturbative inputs to dark matter (DM) annihilation signals. In the presence of singular interaction potentials, the conventional boundary conditions of the Schrödinger equation fail, obfuscating the computation of SE, its physical origin, and its relation to bound states in such potentials. In this work, we clarify the origin of SE in singular potentials in a two-fold manner: using the framework of velocity power counting in non-relativistic effective field theory (NREFT), and via position-space regularization of singular potentials at short distances to compute SE and bound states. We illustrate our findings through the case of pseudoscalar mediators interacting with massive Dirac DM. We find that when such a system arises from a UV-complete theory at weak coupling, no SE is generated. However, in the case of a derivatively coupled pseudoscalar, where the interaction is described by a higher-dimension operator in an effective theory, SE (and bound states) can occur for weak couplings if there is a large hierarchy between the cutoff scale of the effective theory and the dark matter mass. This SE is sensitive to the choice of the UV completion of the potential at short distances, but a non-negligible SE can persist even when the UV physics alone would not generate any SE; we elucidate the interplay of UV and IR physics in this case.

</details>


### [109] [Soft functions for generalised angularity event shapes at NNLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10716)
*Emmet P. Byrne,Jonathan R. Gaunt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化方法，用于计算一类趋向于角度（angularity）的双喷注事件形状在NLO和NNLO精度下的软函数，并给出了数值计算代码，以支持NNLL'阶次的重求和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现对一类趋向角度的双喷注事件形状的NNLL'精度重求和，需补全其NNLO软函数这一缺失环节。

Method: 采用模块化方法，将软函数结构分解为一个快度积分主导部分和一个可数值计算的三维积分剩余部分，并提供相应数值代码。

Result: 成功计算了该类中三个新事件形状族在NNLO精度下的软函数。

Conclusion: 该方法具有通用性和灵活性，可用于未来e+e-对撞机上强子化效应的研究或历史数据的再分析。

Abstract: We consider a class of dijet event shapes which tend to angularity in the forward limit. The factorisation formula for this class of event shapes differs from angularity only in the soft function. The NNLO soft function of an event shape in this class is therefore the only missing ingredient required for NNLL' resummation. We describe a modular approach to the calculation of the soft function for any event shape in this class, at NLO and NNLO. Much of the structure of the soft function can be written in terms of a single rapidity integral. The remainder can be computed as a three-dimensional numerical integral for which we provide a code. To demonstrate the flexibility of our approach, we compute the NNLO soft function for three new families of event shapes in this class. Potential applications include the investigation of hadronisation effects at future $e^+ e^-$ colliders, or through the re-analysis of archived data.

</details>


### [110] [A Colour-Casimir Adjacency Matrix Approach to Fully-Heavy Tetraquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10822)
*M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加权图谱理论的全重四夸克态唯象框架，通过色Casimir因子加权的完全图K4描述四部分子结构，并用单一混合角统一两个色单态二夸克-反二夸克通道；该方法避免了早期拉普拉斯方法导致的质量序倒置问题；利用X(6900)、X(7100)和候选态X(7200)标定参数后，成功预测全底四夸克基态质量为18.7–18.9 GeV，并指出Tcc+在纯色模型中被低估约93 MeV，暗示存在超出紧致四部分子图像的长程分子型动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有拉普拉斯图模型中色吸引力与质量序关系倒置的物理不合理性，并构建更符合QCD色结构的全重四夸克态谱学框架。

Method: 将四价部分子视为完全图K4的顶点，边权由两个色单态通道（3̄⊗3和6⊗6̄）的色Casimir因子决定；物理态为两通道的相干叠加，仅含一个混合角；直接对角化色加权邻接矩阵获得能谱，而非使用拉普拉斯矩阵。

Result: 标定得混合角α=0.740、能量尺度γ=23.2 MeV、有效粲夸克质量mc=1.77 GeV；预测全底四夸克基态位于18.7–18.9 GeV；发现纯色模型对Tcc+的束缚能低估约93 MeV，揭示分子型长程动力学的重要性；指出X(7200)的暂定性及CMS径向激发解释存在的张力。

Conclusion: 所提邻接矩阵方法比拉普拉斯方法更符合物理预期；三参数标定虽非统计独立检验，但外推预测具物理意义；模型凸显紧致四夸克图像的局限性，需引入自旋、轨道自由度及衰变动力学以完善描述。

Abstract: We present a phenomenological framework for fully-heavy tetraquark spectroscopy based on the spectral theory of weighted graphs. The four valence partons are vertices of the complete graph $K_4$, with edge weights determined by colour-Casimir factors in the two colour-singlet diquark-antidiquark channels, $\bar{3}\otimes3$ and $6\otimes\bar{6}$. A physical state is described as a coherent mixture of these channels through one mixing angle. Unlike an earlier Laplacian-based version, we obtain the spectrum by directly diagonalising the colour-weighted adjacency matrix. We show analytically that the two constructions are inequivalent and that the Laplacian reverses the physically expected relation between colour attraction and mass ordering. Using $X(6900)$, $X(7100)$, and the tentative $X(7200)$ to determine the mixing angle, energy scale, and effective charm mass, we find $α=0.740$, $γ=23.2$ MeV, and $m_c=1.77$ GeV. Since three parameters are fixed by three inputs, this calibration is not an independent statistical test. The predictive content comes from applying the same parameters elsewhere: the all-bottom ground state is predicted at 18.7-18.9 GeV, while the colour-only model under-binds $T_{cc}^+$ by about 93 MeV. This discrepancy provides a quantitative indication of long-range molecular dynamics beyond a compact four-parton colour graph. We discuss the tentative nature of $X(7200)$, the tension with the CMS radial-excitation interpretation, and the limitations of a static model without spin, orbital, or decay dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Generalized Chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ with Inelastic Scalar Dark Matter for the LZ 248 keV Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10827)
*Ranjeet Kumar,Hemant Kumar Prajapati*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于广义手性U(1)_{B-L}模型的非弹性标量暗物质场景，以解释LZ实验观测到的约248 keV核反冲事例；该模型通过暗区标量破缺产生两个近简并态，实现吸热型非弹性散射，从而增强高能区信号，并兼顾遗迹丰度与当前实验限制及对撞机探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ合作组最近报道的约248 keV高能核反冲事件（背景仅2.6σ显著性），探索超出传统弹性散射的暗物质相互作用机制。

Method: 构建一个广义手性U(1)_{B-L}模型，其中暗区标量场对称性破缺后产生两个近简并质量态；轻态作为暗物质候选者，通过离对角耦合与Z'玻色子相互作用，实现吸热型非弹性核散射；分析其动力学、遗迹丰度、LZ事例适配性及对撞机信号。

Result: 确定了满足观测遗迹丰度和当前实验约束（含LZ高能事例）的参数空间；揭示了该非弹性散射机制如何压制低速暗物质贡献、增强高速尾部对高能核反冲的贡献；指出Z'玻色子在对撞机上具有可观测前景。

Conclusion: 该非弹性标量暗物质模型为LZ异常事例提供了一个自洽且可检验的理论解释，兼具宇宙学、直接探测与对撞机多方面验证潜力。

Abstract: The high-energy nuclear recoil event with $E_R\simeq248$ keV recently reported by the LZ collaboration, with a background-only significance of $2.6σ$, offers an intriguing window into dark matter scattering beyond the conventional elastic picture. We study an inelastic scalar dark matter scenario within a generalized chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ framework. After symmetry breaking, the dark sector scalar gives rise to two nearly degenerate states with a small mass splitting. The lighter state serves as the dark matter candidate and couples off-diagonally to the $Z'$ boson, leading to endothermic inelastic scattering. The resulting kinematics suppress the contribution from low-velocity dark matter while making the high-velocity tail increasingly relevant for nuclear recoils at higher energies. We identify the parameter space consistent with the observed relic abundance and current experimental constraints, and discuss the inelastic scattering kinematics relevant to the recent LZ observation, together with the complementary collider prospects for the associated $Z'$ boson.

</details>


### [112] [Glueball interactions from the colour Van der Waals potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10841)
*Juan Cánovas Cúneo,Felipe J. Llanes-Estrada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯杨-米尔斯理论中胶球-胶球相互作用，发现色范德华力在中等距离（0.66–1.9 fm）起关键作用，且库仑与线性势贡献部分抵消，导致整体相互作用比仅考虑库仑部分的伦敦力弱得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于胶球存在大的质量间隙，范德华相互作用相比轻夸克强子中由π介子主导的 Yukawa 相互作用更为显著，因此需系统研究其微观机制及对胶球物理的影响。

Method: 基于组分胶子图像，沿伦敦-爱森希茨-王方法计算色范德华势；采用库伦-线性组合的 Cornell 势作为微观色荷间势，分别提取其对范德华力的贡献。

Result: 色范德华势在0.66–1.9 fm范围内显著；Cornell 势中线性部分与库伦部分对基态胶球的范德华贡献近似抵消，使总相互作用明显弱于仅由库伦部分导出的伦敦力。

Conclusion: 胶球相互作用不能仅用类稀释子模型描述，应发展基于范德华机制的有效场论；该结果为理解格点QCD胶球散射数据提供了新视角。

Abstract: We examine pure Yang-Mills theory glueball-glueball interactions in constituent-gluon approaches. Because of the large mass gap, Van der Waals interactions are relatively more significant than in light-quark QCD hadrons where the pion gives rise to strong Yukawa exchanges. We find that the colour Van der Waals potential, computed along the traditional lines of the quantum London-Eisenschitz-Wang force, is a relevant interaction at distances between about 0.66 fm (when the glueballs are in contact) to 1.9 fm (when the virtual string tension among the colour-polarized glueballs in the intermediate state breaks down). In employing a Cornell potential as the microscopic one among colour charges, we find that the Van der Waals interaction derived from the linear potential part closely cancels that of the Coulomb one for the ground state glueballs. This means that the interaction, while sizeable and capable of saturating the lattice data depending on parameters, is far weaker than the London force derived from the Coulombic part alone. (This suggests that one should move effective theories for glueballs beyond dilaton-type approaches and perhaps deploy some variant of Van der Waals Effective Field Theory.)

</details>


### [113] [Axionic Wormholes in Metric-Affine Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11010)
*Shonosuke Takeshita,Naoki Yoshioka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在度规-仿射引力框架下研究轴子虫洞作为显式U(1)对称性破缺的来源，通过非最小耦合到Holst和Nieh-Yan等拓扑项来增强欧几里得虫洞作用量，从而缓解轴子质量等级问题，并找到与暴胀约束相容的参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决轴子质量等级问题，即量子引力效应显式破坏全局U(1)对称性，威胁轴子解决强CP问题的能力。

Method: 在包含挠率和非度规性的度规-仿射引力框架中，引入非最小耦合至Holst和Nieh-Yan等拓扑项，分析其对轴子虫洞动力学及欧氏作用量的影响。

Result: 非最小耦合增强了虫洞作用量，缓解了轴子质量等级问题；双耦合协同可进一步扩大可行参数空间；识别出若干与暴胀约束兼容的典型参数区域。

Conclusion: 度规-仿射引力中的轴子虫洞可通过拓扑项非最小耦合有效缓解轴子质量等级问题，为轴子理论与量子引力相容性提供了新路径。

Abstract: The axion is a promising candidate for solving the strong CP problem. To solve this problem, the global U(1) symmetry must be preserved to a high degree of accuracy. However, it is well known that global symmetries are explicitly violated by quantum gravity effects, giving rise to what is referred to as the axion quality problem. In this paper, we investigate axionic wormholes as a source of explicit U(1) violation in Metric-Affine Gravity. This framework allows for spacetime torsion and non-metricity, which accommodate additional curvature-like and topological terms, such as the Holst and Nieh--Yan terms, that are absent from the metric and Palatini formalisms. We show that non-minimal couplings to these terms modify the wormhole dynamics and enhance the Euclidean wormhole action, thereby alleviating the axion quality problem. We also find that the viable parameter space is enlarged when two of these couplings are simultaneously present. We further identify representative parameter regions where the alleviation of the axion quality problem is compatible with inflationary constraints.

</details>


### [114] [Qubit-Qutrit Quantum Tomography of hadronic $Λφ$ and $ΛK^{\ast 0}$ systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11151)
*Veronica Kanachova,Feng Liu,Zhoudunming Tu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自旋1/2-自旋1（qubit-qutrit）系统的量子层析技术（QQQT），用于分析ΛV（V=φ或K*0）末态，解决了不等自旋强子系统中因强衰变导致部分矢量极化信息丢失而密度矩阵不完整的问题，并建立了基于不完整密度矩阵的纠缠认证精确判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子信息观测量（如纠缠、自旋关联）拓展至不等自旋强子末态（如ΛV）面临根本挑战：其6×6密度矩阵含35个独立自旋参数，但ΛV衰变仅能提供23个可观测参数，因矢量介子强衰变隐藏了部分极化信息。

Method: 构建适用于ΛV（Λφ和ΛK*0）自旋-1/2⊗1系统的qubit-qutrit量子层析（QQQT）方法，并推导出基于不完整密度矩阵（仅23个参数）的严格纠缠认证判据。

Result: 实现了对Λφ和ΛK*0系统的有效量子层析；给出了不完备测量下纠缠存在的精确数学判据；为非微扰QCD强子化过程提供了新探针，可直接比较真空产生的纠缠夸克对在强子化为重子（Λ）或矢量介子（φ/K*0）过程中的自旋演化。

Conclusion: QQQT技术突破了不等自旋强子系统量子态表征的瓶颈，使基于实验可观测量的纠缠认证成为可能，开辟了用量子信息工具研究QCD强子化的全新路径。

Abstract: Quantum-information observables have emerged in recent years as new tools in nuclear and particle physics, from entanglement in top-quark pairs to spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ production. Extending these studies to unequal-spin hadronic final states poses a fundamental challenge: the $6\times6$ density matrix of a qubit-qutrit system contains 35 independent spin parameters, but the decays of $ΛV$ pairs, with $V=φ$ or $K^{*0}$, provide access to only 23 due to the hidden vector polarization from the strong decay. In this Letter, we formulate a qubit-qutrit quantum tomography (QQQT) technique for these spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}\otimes1$ systems and establish exact criteria for entanglement certification from the \textit{incomplete} density matrix. Compared with the $Λ\barΛ$ system, QQQT of $Λφ$ and $ΛK^{*0}$ provides a new probe of nonperturbative QCD hadronization, enabling a direct comparison of the spin evolution of entangled quark pairs produced from the vacuum as they hadronize into a baryon or a vector meson.

</details>


### [115] [Twist decomposition of exclusive heavy meson production cross sections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11179)
*Mariusz Sadzikowski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在BFKL形式下研究了重矢量介子电生和光生的总截面的扭度分解，发现低-x区域高扭度贡献被强烈抑制，并讨论了核靶可能增强高扭度效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重矢量介子电生和光生过程中不同扭度成分对总截面的贡献，特别是在低-x区域的行为特征。

Method: 采用领头对数1/x BFKL形式论，计算矢量介子冲击因子的Mellin变换，并分析扭度分解。

Result: 低-x运动学区域中高扭度贡献被强烈抑制；核靶情形下高扭度效应可能存在增强。

Conclusion: 在γ∗p过程中，扭度展开有效，主导贡献来自低扭度项；核靶为观测高扭度效应提供了潜在可能。

Abstract: We study the twist decomposition of the total cross sections for exclusive heavy vector meson electroproduction and photoproduction in the $γ^\ast p$ processes, within the leading logarithmic $1/x$ BFKL formalism. The Mellin transforms of the impact factors of the vector meson are calculated. We show that the higher twist contributions are strongly suppressed in the low-$x$ kinematical regime. Possible enhancement of the higher twists effects for nuclei targets is discussed.

</details>


### [116] [Doubling down on proton structure: what double parton scattering can and cannot tell us about partonic separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11196)
*Andrew Fowlie,Arthur Moraes,Hao Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双部分子散射中的有效截面σ_eff与质子内部部分子横向分离r之间的关系，推导出⟨r²⟩的严格下界，并证明先前提出的上界不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双部分子散射是探测质子结构的独特手段，其特征参数为有效截面σ_eff；本文旨在探究σ_eff能揭示多少关于部分子横向分离的信息。

Method: 在双部分子散射截面的因子化假设下，不预设横向分布的具体形式，通过理论推导和显式反例分析，研究σ_eff与横向部分子分离r的关系。

Result: 推导出横向部分子分离平方平均值的严格下界⟨r²⟩ ≥ 4σ_eff/(9π)，并构造反例证伪了以往提出的上界。

Conclusion: 在有效截面参数化框架下，σ_eff可提供部分子横向分离的严格下界，但不能给出普适的上界。

Abstract: Double parton scattering provides a unique probe of proton structure and is commonly characterized by the effective cross section $σ_\mathrm{eff}$. We examine, under the factorized ansatz for the double-parton-scattering cross section and without assuming any specific parametric form for the transverse profile, what the effective cross section can reveal about the transverse separation, $r$, between partons in the proton. We derive a sharp lower bound on the transverse partonic separation, $\langle r^2\rangle \ge 4σ_\mathrm{eff}/(9π)$, and demonstrate through explicit counterexamples that previously posited upper bounds do not hold. Our results establish, within the effective-cross-section parametrization of double parton scattering framework, that $σ_\mathrm{eff}$ can provide a sharp lower bound on partonic separation but cannot provide a general upper bound.

</details>


### [117] [Higgsino dark matter in the Starobinsky supergravity with the MSSM in light of the LUX-ZEPLIN event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11241)
*Daniel Frolovsky,Sergei V. Ketov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Starobinsky超引力耦合的最小超对称标准模型中实现了一个质量约1 TeV、近乎纯higgsino的暗物质候选者，并将粒子谱（包括希格斯质量、higgsino质量及其劈裂）与宇宙暴胀观测量通过引力介导的超对称破缺及重整化群演化相联系；结果表明，bino诱导劈裂下希格斯质量与实验值一致，而wino诱导劈裂被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到248 keV核反冲事件，重新激起了对higgsino暗物质的研究兴趣。

Method: 基于Starobinsky超引力耦合的最小超对称标准模型，利用引力介导的超对称破缺机制和后续重整化群演化，将粒子谱与宇宙暴胀观测量关联。

Result: 模型预测的希格斯玻色子质量在bino诱导质量劈裂下与实验值在误差范围内一致；而wino诱导劈裂导致希格斯质量偏高数GeV，因而被排除。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了近纯higgsino暗物质候选者，并通过暴胀与粒子物理参数的关联，对超对称破缺机制给出了可检验的约束。

Abstract: We realize a nearly pure higgsino dark matter candidate with mass of about 1 TeV in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model coupled to the Starobinsky supergravity. The recent LUX-ZEPLIN 248 keV nuclear-recoil event has renewed interest in higgsino dark matter. In this framework, the particle spectrum, including the Higgs boson mass, the higgsino mass and its splitting, is connected to cosmic inflation observables via gravitational mediation of supersymmetry breaking and subsequent renormalization group evolution. We show that the Higgs boson mass predicted by the model agrees with the measured value within the quoted uncertainties for a broad range of bino-induced mass splittings. Wino-induced splitting leads to a Higgs mass several GeV higher and is therefore excluded.

</details>


### [118] [Relocating the SIMP Miracle in the Axion Portal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11254)
*Nakorn Thongyoi,Chakrit Pongkitivanichkul*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了SIMP（强相互作用大质量粒子）暗物质场景下的轴子门户模型，发现标准动能接触机制被亚GeV轴子类粒子（ALP）实验限制所排除；暗区在自身温度下冻结，导致遗迹丰度不再固定自相互作用截面，而是预测门户耦合强度；由于门户算符为厄米且偶函数，不产生三线性ALP-π耦合，导致ππ→aa过程远快于预期，从而排除仅靠暗凝聚态生成ALP质量的最简实现；可行模型要求ALP略重于暗π介子，由此确定味子真空期望值约为1.1×10¹⁰ GeV，并预言暗物质自相互作用截面约为0.20 cm²/g（对应暗π介子质量140 MeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SIMP暗物质模型在轴子门户框架下与亚GeV ALP实验约束的兼容性问题，并厘清遗迹丰度、自相互作用与门户耦合之间的关系。

Method: 理论分析与有效场论匹配：构建包含ALP、暗π介子和味子的低能有效拉氏量；考虑WZW型3→2湮灭过程；计算ππ→aa散射截面；结合宇宙学丰度、实验约束（如K⁺→π⁺a）及自然性要求筛选可行参数空间。

Result: 标准动能接触机制被排除；可行模型要求ALP质量略大于暗π介子；味子真空期望值被固定为~1.1×10¹⁰ GeV；预言暗物质自相互作用截面为0.20 cm²/g（暗π质量140 MeV，允许范围82–169 MeV）；预测能力从自相互作用转移到味子能标。

Conclusion: SIMP框架的预测力未丧失，而是从暗物质自相互作用迁移至味子能标；该模型在满足所有现有约束的同时，给出可检验的唯象信号。

Abstract: In the strongly interacting massive particle (SIMP) scenario, dark matter is a pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone boson whose abundance is set by a three-to-two Wess--Zumino--Witten process. We study this scenario in an axion portal and find that the kinetic contact the canonical mechanism assumes is excluded by bounds on sub-GeV axion-like particles (ALPs). The dark sector then freezes out at its own temperature, so the relic abundance no longer fixes the self-interaction cross section but predicts the portal coupling instead. Moreover, the portal operator is Hermitian and even in the ALP field, so no trilinear ALP--pion coupling arises. The contact term then drives $ππ\to aa$ with nothing to cancel against it, and we find the conversion four orders of magnitude faster than a trilinear estimate gives. This disfavours the minimal realisation in which the dark condensate alone generates the ALP mass. The realisation that survives instead makes the ALP slightly heavier than the dark pion. Matching the observed abundance then fixes the flavon vacuum expectation value at $V_φ\simeq 1.1\times10^{10} \ {\rm GeV}$ and leaves the dark scale open over more than an order of magnitude. The decay $K^+\toπ^+a$ requires the flavon to charge the leptons alone. The model then predicts a dark matter self-interaction of $0.20\,{\rm cm^2/g}$ at a dark pion mass of $140\ {\rm MeV}$, in a window running from $82$ to $169 \ {\rm MeV}$. The predictive power of the SIMP framework is therefore not lost but relocated, from the self-interaction to the flavon scale.

</details>


### [119] [Broad p-Wave Sommerfeld Resonances From Dimensional Deconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11305)
*Nobuki Yoshimatsu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究维度解构是否能定性地改变长程力，发现五维反德西特时空的解构可有效产生平方反比势，从而自然引发宽p波Sommerfeld共振，并将其应用于暗物质现象学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索维度解构对长程力的定性影响，尤其是其在暗物质物理中的潜在应用。

Method: 理论分析五维AdS时空的维度解构，推导其产生的有效势并研究对应的Sommerfeld共振行为。

Result: 维度解构可自然生成平方反比势，并导致宽p波Sommerfeld共振，无需精细调节。

Conclusion: 维度解构为解释暗物质相关现象（如增强的湮灭截面）提供了一种无需微调的新机制。

Abstract: We investigate whether dimensional deconstruction can qualitatively modify long-range forces. We show that the deconstruction of a five-dimensional AdS spacetime effectively generates an inverse-square potential, which naturally gives rise to broad p-wave Sommerfeld resonances without fine-tuning. We then apply this mechanism to dark matter phenomenology.

</details>


### [120] [Connecting Vector-like Muons, pNGB Dark Matter and Electroweak Phase Transition through Collider and Gravitational Wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11386)
*Jaydeb Das,Saurabh Niyogi,Amir Subba*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个扩展标准模型，引入两个带Z2和Z3对称性的复标量单态及一个μ子亲和的矢量型轻子，构建了一个由赝戈德斯通玻色子（pNGB）主导的暗物质模型，并探讨其在直接探测、对撞机物理与引力波信号中的互补探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗物质候选者与实验约束（如直接探测截面、LHC限制）之间的张力，并探索多探针互补性（对撞机、相变引力波、暗物质探测）。

Method: 构建含Z2/Z3对称性标量单态和矢量型μ子的扩展模型；分析赝戈德斯通暗物质的产生机制、稳定性、散射截面（树图与圈图）、热遗存丰度；开展3 TeV缪子对撞机多变量分析；计算强一阶电弱相变与相应随机引力波谱。

Result: 获得满足暗物质丰度、LHC单喷注、当前直接探测上限的可行参数空间；一环DM-核子散射截面低于当前极限但未来可测；缪子对撞机中t-道标量交换显著增强矢量型μ子产率；识别出能产生可观测引力波信号的强一阶相变基准点。

Conclusion: 该模型实现了pNGB暗物质、缪子对撞机新物理探针与电弱相变引力波信号的自然统一，凸显多信使探测的互补价值。

Abstract: We study an extension of the Standard Model with two complex scalar singlets, $S_2$ and $S_3$, charged under $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and $\mathbb{Z}_3$, respectively, and a muon-philic vector-like lepton $ψ$ carrying the same $\mathbb{Z}_3$ charge as $S_3$. The pseudoscalar associated with the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ sector remains stable due to the CP symmetry of the potential and serves as the dark matter (DM) candidate, with its mass generated through the corresponding soft breaking of the global $U(1)$ symmetry. The vector-like muon couples to the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ scalar and renders the second pseudoscalar unstable, thereby realizing an effectively single-component pNGB DM scenario. The pNGB nature strongly suppresses the tree-level spin-independent direct-detection cross section, while viable parameter regions reproduce the observed relic abundance and satisfy LHC monojet constraints. We further compute the one-loop contribution to DM-nucleon scattering and find that the resulting cross section remains below current experimental limits while being potentially accessible to future direct-detection experiments. At a multi-TeV muon collider, scalar mediated $ t$-channel processes can significantly enhance vector-like-muon pair production. We perform a detailed multivariate analysis for a future muon collider at 3 TeV center-of-mass energy. We also identify viable benchmark points exhibiting strong first-order electroweak phase transitions (SFOEWPT) with successful bubble nucleation, which can generate potentially observable stochastic gravitational wave (GW) signals. These results highlight the complementarity of dark matter searches, muon-collider probes, and SFOEWPT, with the resulting GW signals providing an additional probe of the extended scalar sector.

</details>


### [121] [Net-baryon number distributions and the QCD phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11483)
*R. N. Rogalyov,N. V. Gerasimeniuk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一种直接从净质子多重数分布中确定重子化学势μ_B的方法，并验证了其与热统计模型和强子共振气体（HRG）模型结果的一致性，同时提出了重核碰撞中火球热化的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确确定重离子碰撞中产生的火球的重子化学势μ_B，并检验其热化程度。

Method: 采用大正则系综方法分析STAR合作组测得的净质子多重数分布，并首次应用一种直接从中提取μ_B的方法。

Result: 所得μ_B值与热统计模型及HRG模型拟合结果一致，并据此提出了火球热化的判据。

Conclusion: 该方法有效且自洽，为重离子碰撞中热力学参数的提取提供了新途径，并支持火球在特定条件下达到热平衡。

Abstract: Net-proton multiplicity distributions obtained by the STAR collaboration are analyzed in the grand canonical approach. A method of finding the baryon chemical potential $μ_B$ immediately from such distributions is applied for the first time. The obtained values of $μ_B$ are consistent both with those determined earlier using the statistical thermal model and with the results of the fit based on the Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG) model. We propose a criterion of thermalization of fireballs produced in heavy-nuclei collisions.

</details>


### [122] [Cosmic ray boosted dark matter with momentum dependent interactions can explain the LZ 248 keV event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11600)
*Matti Heikinheimo,Niklas Zimmermann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV的核反冲事件，本文提出该事件可能源于宇宙线加速的高速暗物质，并在非相对论有效理论框架下分析其反冲谱，指出动量依赖型相互作用算符（如O6和O10）可产生与观测能量吻合的谱峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中意外观测到的高能（248 keV）单核反冲事件，该能量区本预期无背景事件，需探索新物理机制，特别是宇宙线加速暗物质的可能性。

Method: 在非相对论有效场论框架下，建模宇宙线散射产生的高速暗物质流，计算其在探测器中诱导的核反冲能谱，重点分析动量依赖型相互作用算符（O6和O10）对谱形的影响。

Result: 发现O6和O10等动量依赖算符可产生在~248 keV处出现峰值的核反冲谱，与LZ观测事件能量一致，从而为该异常事件提供一种可行的暗物质物理解释。

Conclusion: LZ实验的高能单事件可能源自宇宙线加速的暗物质，且其相互作用可通过特定动量依赖算符实现；该解释虽具可能性，但仍需更多数据或实验验证。

Abstract: The LZ experiment has recently reported an observation of a single event with characteristics of a nuclear recoil at a high recoil energy of 248 keV, where no background events are expected. We show that such nuclear recoil event could be caused by high velocity dark matter particles originating from interactions with energetic cosmic rays. We analyze the expected recoil spectrum due to cosmic ray boosted dark matter within the non-relativistic effective theory formalism, and show that momentum-dependent operators, such as $\mathcal{O}_6$ and $\mathcal{O}_{10}$, can produce a spectrum that peaks at the observed energy.

</details>


### [123] [Collinear fragmentation of pseudoscalar quarkonia from NLO NRQCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11604)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto,Francesca Lonigro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NRFF1.0——一套新的公开可用的赝标量夸克偶素共线碎裂函数（FFs），基于HF-NRevo方案，结合NRQCD理论框架，在初始能标计算FF输入并进行演化，旨在为中高横动量区域的重夸克偶素产生提供高精度基准，并适用于研究喷注内碎裂、喷注淬火、能量损失及夸克胶子等离子体中的介质修正碎裂模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克偶素产生涉及从重夸克对产生到束缚态形成的强相互作用全过程，需兼顾微扰与非微扰QCD动力学；现有碎裂函数缺乏对中高横动量区精确描述及对喷注结构和介质效应的系统建模能力。

Method: 基于Heavy-Flavor Non-Relativistic evolution (HF-NRevo) 方案，以Non-Relativistic QCD (NRQCD) 为理论基础在初始能标计算碎裂函数输入，再通过HF-NRevo进行演化；显式处理各部分子通道与重味阈值。

Result: 构建了NRFF1.0碎裂函数集，为中至高横动量区重夸克偶素产生提供了高精度基准，支持对喷注内碎裂、喷注淬火敏感性、能量损失机制及夸克胶子等离子体中介质修正碎裂模式的定量研究。

Conclusion: NRFF1.0为重夸克偶素产生提供了兼具理论自洽性与实验适用性的新工具，显著提升了在强子对撞与重离子碰撞中解析碎裂过程与介质效应的能力。

Abstract: We discuss a new family of publicly available collinear fragmentation functions (FFs) for pseudoscalar quarkonia, the NRFF1.0 set. It builds upon the Heavy-Flavor Non-Relativistic evolution (HF-NRevo) scheme, designed to describe heavy-hadron formation through leading-power fragmentation at moderate and large transverse momentum. Heavy-quarkonium production naturally involves both perturbative and non-perturbative QCD dynamics, from the production of the heavy $Q\bar{Q}$ pair to its formation into a physical bound state. Within NRFF1.0, Non-Relativistic Quantum Chromodynamics (NRQCD) provides the theoretical framework for calculating the FF inputs at the initial scale, which are subsequently evolved through the HF-NRevo scheme. This setup provides a precision baseline for investigating the underlying partonic hierarchy and jet structure across the moderate- to high-transverse-momentum regime. The explicit treatment of partonic channels and heavy-flavor thresholds makes this framework particularly suitable for exploring quarkonium-in-jet fragmentation, jet-quenching sensitivity, energy-loss mechanisms, and the emergence of medium-modified fragmentation patterns in the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [124] [Distinguishing a pseudoscalar from a vector $t\bar t$ resonance with top-quark spin correlations at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11641)
*Ahmed Bellagroudi,Farida Fassi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过顶夸克自旋关联研究CP奇性两希格斯二重态模型赝标量A与自旋-1轻子禁戒topcolor Z'粒子的区分能力，在双轻子末态中分析其部分子层次螺旋度关联差异及实验可分辨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用顶夸克自旋关联作为共振态ttbar衰变量子数的直接探针，以区分具有不同自旋和CP性质的新物理模型。

Method: 比较CP奇赝标量A与自旋-1 Z'在双轻子道的部分子螺旋度关联差异（Δchel≈0.43），结合中微子加权重建、信号归一化策略、统计分离度计算（含系统误差处理如高斯约束的SM形状不确定度）及局部模板外推法。

Result: 在3000 fb⁻¹积分亮度下，LO ttbar投影给出2.27–2.74σ期望区分度；纯形状分析得1.86–2.70σ；考虑SM chel形状系统误差后为1.67–2.63σ；局部固定模板外推可达5σ区分，对应耦合增强约16–22%；1500 GeV点单独给出。

Conclusion: 区分能力受限于极低信噪比（亚百分比信号份额）及质量依赖的SM自旋形状控制精度，而非共振态本征自旋差异本身。

Abstract: Top-quark spin correlations provide a direct probe of the quantum numbers of a resonance decaying to $t\bar t$. We compare a CP-odd type-II two-Higgs-doublet-model pseudoscalar $A$ with a spin-1 leptophobic topcolor $Z'$ in the dilepton channel. The two hypotheses differ in the parton-level helicity correlation by $Δc_{\rm hel}\simeq0.43$, nearly independently of mass, and neutrino-weighted reconstruction preserves this separation with an effective dilution factor of about 0.66. At 400 and 800 GeV, the pseudoscalar normalization is anchored to CMS HIG-22-013 coupling limits, while the $Z'$ rate is matched to the pseudoscalar so that the comparison primarily tests spin structure. The leading-order $t\bar t$-only projection gives expected separations of 2.27 and 2.74 standard deviations at $3000\,{\rm fb}^{-1}$. Equalizing the selected signal yields gives pure spin-shape separations of 1.86 and 2.70 standard deviations. Profiling a Gaussian-constrained Standard Model $c_{\rm hel}$-shape nuisance gives 2.07 and 2.63 standard deviations for an optimistic sideband benchmark, or 1.67 and 2.26 for a weaker constraint. A local fixed-template extrapolation corresponds to five-standard-deviation discrimination at couplings about 22\% and 16\% above the reference values. A 1500 GeV $g=1$ point is shown separately. The reach is limited by the sub-percent signal fraction and control of the mass-dependent Standard Model spin shape, rather than by the intrinsic resonance-spin separation.

</details>


### [125] [An event generator for $h\to b\bar{b}$ decays at NLO in SMEFT matched to a parton shower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11681)
*Livia E. G. Maskos,Benjamin D. Pecjak,Shakeel Ur Rahaman,Marek Schönherr*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了在Sherpa事件生成器中对标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）下希格斯玻色子衰变h→b b̄过程的NLO修正，包括NLO QCD和弱虚修正，并与部分子喷注匹配，支持结合产生过程进行完整LHC模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将SMEFT框架下的高阶修正纳入主流蒙特卡洛事件生成器（如Sherpa），以支持具有真实末态和实验可接受相空间截断的精确唯象研究。

Method: 基于MCatNLO型框架实现SMEFT中h→b b̄衰变的NLO QCD修正；引入不同电弱输入方案下的两体矩阵元形式的弱虚修正；所有事例均通过QCD部分子喷注演化；并将该衰变模块嵌入Zh关联产生过程以验证可行性。

Result: 给出了包含SMEFT算符贡献的h→b b̄衰变总宽度及静止系微分分布的数值结果；成功将SMEFT NLO衰变模块与独立产生的Zh产生样本耦合，在Sherpa中完成端到端模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在Sherpa中实现了SMEFT NLO衰变修正，为在现实实验条件下开展SMEFT唯象学研究（如带运动学截断、探测器效应等）提供了可靠工具基础，并可推广至其他过程。

Abstract: We present a Sherpa implementation of NLO corrections in dimension-six Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) to the benchmark process $h\to b\bar b$. The calculation includes NLO QCD corrections matched to a parton shower using an MCatNLO-type framework for decays. Virtual weak effects are included as two-body matrix-element corrections in different electroweak input schemes, with the corresponding decay events also evolved with the QCD parton shower. We present numerical results for the inclusive decay rate and for differential distributions in the Higgs-boson rest frame, and illustrate how the decay implementation can be combined with an independently generated production sample by embedding it in associated $Zh$ production at the LHC. This proof-of-principle implementation demonstrates how NLO SMEFT corrections can be incorporated into the widely used Sherpa event generator, providing a basis for extensions to other processes and for SMEFT studies with realistic final states and fiducial cuts within that framework.

</details>


### [126] [Hunting the Unseen: Deep Learning Analysis for Semi-Visible Jet Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11692)
*Miguel A. Avendaño-Bernal,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Ahmed Hammad,Stefano Moretti,Mihoko Nojiri,Michael H. Seymour,Claire Shepherd-Themistocleous*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了半可见喷注（SVJs）这一强相互作用暗区的独特对撞机信号，通过结合全局运动学与喷注子结构观测量（如Lund Jet Plane、能量关联函数等），训练了五种深度学习分类器以区分SVJ与标准喷注，并发现融合多源信息可显著提升判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVJs是强相互作用暗区的重要探测信号，包含可见标准模型粒子和不可见暗强子，具有相关喷注活动与缺失横动量特征；但其复杂内部结构尚未被充分挖掘，需发展更精细的判别方法。

Method: 采用多种喷注子结构观测量（Lund Jet Plane图像、两体能量关联、角性、带电强子多重数）与全局事件运动学结合，构建五类深度学习模型：Vision Transformer（处理LJP图像）、JetLOV（基于层次聚类树）、MLP（使用高阶观测量）、以及两种多模态网络（融合图像/层次表征与高阶观测量）。

Result: 全局运动学观测量判别能力最强，层次聚类表征优于LJP图像；融合全局运动学与子结构信息获得最优整体性能。

Conclusion: 仅依赖全局运动学不足以充分挖掘SVJs的发现潜力，必须结合其内部辐射模式与聚类结构等丰富子结构信息，为LHC未来SVJ搜索提供重要基准。

Abstract: Semi-Visible Jets (SVJs) constitute a distinctive collider signature of strongly interacting dark sectors, embedding Dark Matter candidates, wherein jets contain both visible Standard Model objects and invisible dark hadrons, giving rise to correlated jet activity and missing transverse momentum. In this work, we investigate SVJs produced through a heavy Z' mediator and perform an study over a representative set of benchmark scenarios spanning different mediator masses and dark sector parameters in the context of so-called Hidden Valley Models. To characterise the signal, we combine global event kinematics with jet substructure observables, including the primary Lund Jet Plane (LJP), the two-point energy correlation, angularity, and charged hadron multiplicity. These representations are used to train five Deep Learning classifiers for SVJ vs standard jet discrimination: a Vision Transformer operating on LJP images, a JetLOV network based on a hierarchical clustering tree, a Multi-Layer Perceptron using high level observables, and two multimodal networks that combine the image-based or hierarchical representations of the radiation pattern with the high jet-level observables. This enables a direct combination of global kinematics, radiation patterns, and jet clustering structure. We find that global kinematic observables outperform the LJP and hierarchical jet representations, with the latter providing stronger discrimination than LJP images. Combining these complementary representations with global kinematics yields the best overall performance. More broadly, this study shows that unlocking the full discovery potential of SVJs would benefit from going beyond global kinematics to exploit the rich information encoded in their internal structure, providing a benchmark for future searches at the Large Hadron Collider.

</details>


### [127] [Might the radiation era extend back to the Big Bang? On dark matter production and the relic graviton background in quadratic gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11750)
*Latham Boyle,Neil Turok,Vatsalya Vaibhav*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二次引力理论框架下重新审视了暗物质和热引力子背景的产生问题，提出了一个新的引力产生机制，并建立了暗物质质量与理论参数之间的新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典爱因斯坦引力预言辐射时期回溯至大爆炸会导致暗物质过量及热引力子背景，但该结论在更基本的二次引力理论中可能不成立。

Method: 在包含R²和Weyl张量平方项的二次引力（最小可重整化引力）框架下，分析辐射主导时期早期宇宙演化对暗物质丰度和引力子背景的影响。

Result: 发现暗物质质量m_dm与Weyl项系数f₂存在新关系，可解释观测暗物质丰度；同时，遗迹引力子背景是否可观测将约束参数f₀和f₂。

Conclusion: 二次引力提供了一种新的引力主导的暗物质产生机制（如稳定右手中微子），并使遗迹热引力子背景成为检验该理论的关键观测探针。

Abstract: Naive estimates based on Einstein gravity with Lagrangian $\frac{1}{2}M_{pl}^{2}(R-2Λ)$ suggest that, if the radiation era extended back to the Big Bang (i.e., as far back as the classical spacetime background makes sense), this would result in an over-production of dark matter and a relic cosmic background of thermal gravitons. Here we revisit these conclusions in quadratic gravity, the minimal renormalizable completion of Einstein gravity, whose Lagrangian also includes the terms $\frac{1}{6}f_0^{-2}R^2-\frac{1}{2}f_{2}^{-2}C^{2}$. Assuming the radiation era does extend back to the bang, we find a novel relation between the coefficient $f_{2}$ and the dark matter mass $m_{dm}$, needed to obtain the correct dark matter abundance. This yields a new gravitational production mechanism for dark matter (e.g., stable right-handed neutrinos). Moreover, the presence or absence of the relic graviton background (detectable by forthcoming CMB experiments via its small imprint on $N_{\rm eff}$) will place new constraints on $f_{0}, f_{2}$.

</details>


### [128] [NLO corrections to the NEik DIS structure functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11700)
*Tolga Altinoluk,Guillaume Beuf,Jules Favrel,Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文总结了深度非弹性散射（DIS）结构函数中，次领头阶（NLO）对次偶极（NEik）夸克背景贡献的修正。在NEik精度下，除来自靶标胶子背景场的修正外，还存在由靶标夸克背景场代表的t通道夸克交换贡献，这是NEik精度下最低阶的α_s贡献。研究发现，纵向NEik结构函数的NLO修正是有限的，而横向NEik结构函数的NLO修正则表现出快度和紫外（UV）发散，并对其进行了分析与有限部分提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度非弹性散射中，为更精确描述高能极限下的核子结构，需考虑次偶极（NEik）近似下的高阶量子色动力学（QCD）修正，尤其是夸克背景场贡献的NLO修正，以完善理论预测并理解发散结构。

Method: 采用QCD微扰论框架，在次偶极（NEik）近似下系统计算DIS结构函数中夸克背景场贡献的NLO修正；通过正规化方法分析并分离快度与紫外发散，提取有限物理部分。

Result: 发现纵向NEik结构函数的NLO修正为有限值；横向NEik结构函数的NLO修正含快度与紫外发散，已成功识别并提取其有限部分。

Conclusion: NEik精度下夸克背景场的NLO修正具有显著的发散结构差异（纵向有限、横向发散），该结果对高能QCD演化方程及小x物理建模具有重要启示，强调需谨慎处理横向通道中的正规化方案。

Abstract: We summarize the next-to-leading order (NLO) corrections to next-to-eikonal (NEik) quark background contributions to DIS structure functions. At NEik accuracy, in addition to corrections arising from the gluon background field of the target, DIS structure functions receive contributions from the $t$-channel quark exchanges, represented by insertions of the quark background field of the target. The latter provide the lowest order contributions in $α_s$ at NEik accuracy. We show that the NLO corrections to the longitudinal NEik structure functions are finite, whereas those to the transverse NEik structure functions exhibit rapidity and ultraviolet (UV) divergences. We analyze these divergences and extract the finite contributions.

</details>


### [129] [Precise bubble wall velocity in a specific phase transition pattern in the CxSM and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11747)
*Shihang Tang,Fa Peng Huang,Chikako Idegawa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙学一级相变中气泡壁速度的自洽确定方法，以标准模型的复单态扩展为例，结合玻尔兹曼输运方程与流体力学分析，通过稳态力平衡条件数值计算气泡壁速度，并探讨其对重子不对称性等早期宇宙现象的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡壁速度是宇宙学一级相变中的关键物理量，影响电弱重子生成、引力波信号、暗物质丰度和原初黑洞形成等，但现有研究常将其作为自由输入，缺乏自洽计算。

Method: 以复单态扩展的标准模型为例，采用玻尔兹曼输运方程计算微观粒子摩擦，结合流体力学描述宏观等离子体响应，并通过稳态力平衡条件数值求解气泡壁速度。

Result: 发现气泡壁速度由有效势驱动与等离子体摩擦的竞争决定，其变化显著影响重子不对称性；首次对该类相变模式下的气泡动力学进行了定量研究。

Conclusion: 该工作为一类被忽视的相变模式提供了气泡壁动力学的定量分析框架，提升了早期宇宙相关唯象研究的自洽性和预测能力。

Abstract: The bubble wall velocity is a key quantity in cosmological first-order phase transitions, with important implications for electroweak baryogenesis, gravitational wave signals, the dark matter relic density and primordial black holes formed during the phase transition, and so on. However, it is often treated as a free input in phenomenological studies, while a self-consistent determination remains challenging. In this work, taking the complex singlet extension of the Standard Model as an example, we investigate the bubble wall dynamics and velocity in a specific electroweak phase transition pattern where both the Higgs field and the coupled singlet scalar experience friction. The microscopic friction arising from particle interactions with the plasma is evaluated using Boltzmann transport equations, while the macroscopic plasma response is described through hydrodynamic analysis. By applying the steady state force balance condition, we numerically determine the bubble wall velocity for different model parameters. We show that the wall velocity is governed by the competition between the driving force from the effective potential and plasma friction, and that its variation can significantly affect the baryon asymmetry. Our study provides a quantitative investigation of bubble wall dynamics in this overlooked phase transition pattern and its implications for early Universe phenomenology.

</details>


### [130] [Massless-Massive Amplitude Correspondence III: Massive Amplitude Bases in the SMEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11767)
*Yu-Han Ni,Hao Sun,Yi-Ning Wang,Chao Wu,Jiang-Hao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了无质量接触振幅与对称性自发破缺后有质量接触振幅之间的系统对应关系，利用自旋横向（ST）有质量振幅基和最小手征性-螺旋度（MHC）振幅展开，将无质量洛伦兹结构的半标准杨表构造推广至有质量振幅，并应用于SMEFT电弱单味扇区至维度八。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述对称性破缺前后有效理论中的接触振幅，建立无质量与有质量振幅间的系统映射，弥补现有方法在高维算符和多粒子过程中的不足。

Method: 引入自旋横向（ST）有质量振幅基，结合最小手征性-螺旋度（MHC）高能展开；利用U(2)=SU(2)×U(1)_t对称性，将无质量洛伦兹结构的半标准杨表方法推广至有质量情形；分类处理MHC主导项是否具有无质量极限的情形，对五类例外ST振幅通过守恒流耦合匹配。

Result: 给出了SMEFT单味电弱扇区至维度八、3–8个外线粒子的所有接触振幅中，未破缺相Wilson系数与破缺相ST振幅系数之间的显式对应关系；识别出五类需特殊处理的例外ST振幅。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无质量与有质量有效振幅的系统性对接，为EFT在对称性破缺背景下的振幅分析与紫外匹配提供了普适工具，尤其适用于高维算符及多粒子散射过程。

Abstract: We develop a systematic correspondence between massless contact amplitudes in an unbroken theory and massive contact amplitudes after spontaneous symmetry breaking. Our construction employs the spin-transversality (ST) massive amplitude basis, with the systematic high energy expansion through minimal-helicity-chirality (MHC) amplitudes. The resulting $U(2)=SU(2)\times U(1)_t$ description of a massive particle makes the semi-standard Young-tableau construction of massless Lorentz structures directly applicable to massive amplitudes. When the leading-order MHC component has a massless contact limit, it is one-to-one matched directly to its UV amplitude. Otherwise, five exceptional classes of ST amplitudes are identified, their first non-zero descendant components are matched through conserved current couplings to the massless contact amplitude. We apply the framework to the one-flavor electroweak sector of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) through dimension eight, obtaining explicit relations between unbroken-phase Wilson coefficients and broken-phase ST amplitude coefficients for amplitudes with three to eight external particles.

</details>


### [131] [Solar Neutrino Constraints on Inelastic Dark Matter Scattering in Light of Recent LUX-ZEPLIN Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11833)
*Thong T. Q. Nguyen,Tim Linden,Dan Hooper*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了LUX-ZEPLIN实验中观测到的高能核反冲事例所暗示的非弹性暗物质模型，并利用IceCube对太阳高能中微子的观测结果对其参数空间施加强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验观测到一个高能核反冲事例，但缺乏低能事例，提示暗物质与原子核散射可能存在动量依赖或非弹性过程，从而压制低能事例率。

Method: 分析非弹性暗物质被太阳捕获后湮灭产生高能中微子的过程，并结合IceCube未观测到太阳方向高能中微子超出的结果，对相关模型参数进行限制。

Result: IceCube对太阳高能中微子的零超出结果对非弹性暗物质模型（尤其是解释LZ事例的参数区域）构成强约束，显著压缩其可行参数空间。

Conclusion: 太阳中微子观测是检验高能核反冲异常事件所启发的非弹性暗物质模型的关键探针，当前IceCube数据已排除大量此前允许的参数区域。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration recently reported the detection of a single nuclear recoil candidate event with a very high recoil energy. The lack of any corresponding low-energy events motivates models in which dark matter scattering with nuclei has a nontrivial momentum dependence or proceeds inelastically, suppressing the rate of low-energy recoils. In this study, we consider the constraints on inelastic dark matter, including scenarios favored by the LZ event, from the absence of an excess of high-energy neutrinos from the Sun in IceCube observations. We confirm the results of Pospelov & Ramani and show, more generally, that the lack of an excess of high-energy neutrinos from the Sun strongly constrains the parameter space in this class of models.

</details>


### [132] [Signatures of Invisible Fermions in $Λ_b^0 \to Λ_c^+ \ell^- \bar{X}_{\rm inv}$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11840)
*Shantanu Sahoo,Soumitra Nandi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过模型无关的弱有效理论和全面的角分析，研究了重子衰变Λ_b^0 → Λ_c^+ ℓ^- X̄_inv 中大质量不可见费米子的影响，发现不可见粒子质量会在角观测量上留下特征印记，从而区分其质量是否为零，并有效鉴别不同洛伦兹结构（矢量/轴矢量、标量/赝标量、张量）及手征流类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分不可见粒子（如暗物质候选者）的质量是否为零，并甄别新物理中不同类型的相互作用算符。

Method: 基于模型无关的弱有效理论，对Λ_b^0 → Λ_c^+ ℓ^- X̄_inv 衰变开展全面角分布分析。

Result: 发现不可见粒子非零质量会在多个角观测量中产生可观测的特征效应；这些观测量能显著区分质量为零与非零情形，并可强效分辨矢量/轴矢量、标量/赝标量、张量耦合，以及左右手夸克与轻子流。

Conclusion: 角观测量是探测不可见粒子质量及新物理相互作用洛伦兹结构的有力探针，为B物理实验提供了明确的观测策略。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of a massive invisible fermion in $Λ_b^0 \to Λ_c^+ \ell^- \bar{X}_{\rm inv}$ decays using a model-independent weak effective theory. Through a comprehensive angular analysis, we show that a nonzero invisible-particle mass leaves characteristic imprints on angular observables that can distinguish massless and massive invisible states. We further demonstrate that these observables provide strong discrimination among vector/axial-vector, scalar/pseudoscalar, and tensor interactions, as well as between left- and right-handed quark and lepton current operators.

</details>


### [133] [Mathematical inverse problem for the world data inference of the parton distribution functions of the proton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11896)
*Henri Hänninen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性张量重建逆问题的全新方法，用于从深度非弹性散射数据中无模型偏差地约束质子部分子分布函数（PDFs），通过求解由微扰QCD和全局分析定义的耦合线性积分方程组，实现PDF的间接测量式提取，并更稳健地估计不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统PDF拟合中因模型参数化引入的系统性偏差，寻求一种更符合数学逆问题精神、更少依赖先验函数形式的无偏推断方法。

Method: 将PDF约束问题重新表述为线性张量重建逆问题；构建并求解由NLO精度下各类DIS过程（光子、中性流、带电流、重味、中微子散射）所导出的耦合线性泛函积分方程组。

Result: 建立了该逆问题框架的原理性方法，验证了其数学可行性，并展示了其在实现模型无关PDF提取及更鲁棒不确定性估计方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法为PDF全局分析提供了一条摆脱模型参数化偏倚的新路径，在严格遵循微扰QCD框架的同时，向真正意义上的间接测量迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: We show that the inference problem of constraining the parton distribution functions of the proton from deeply inelastic scattering data can be formulated as a linear tensor reconstruction inverse problem. This means that instead of fitting model parameters to the world data, a reconstructive approach to solve a system of coupled linear functional integral equations can be formulated. We leverage the mathematical structures of the integral equations defined by perturbative QCD and global analysis to pose the problem of global analysis as a coupled system of linear integral equations, and construct a proof-of-principle methodology to solve it. To formulate this approach concretely, we review all next-to-leading order accuracy results for inclusive virtual photon, neutral current, charged current, heavy flavor production, and neutrino deep-inelastic lepton--proton scattering. This mathematical methodology of inference opens a path towards a model-bias-free extraction of the proton PDFs from the world data of deeply inelastic scattering in the spirit of indirect measurement employed in mathematical inverse problems, including robust estimation of uncertainties with reduced bias from model parametrization, while closely adhering to the established paradigm of perturbative QCD and global analysis.

</details>


### [134] [Anomalous Dimensions, Matching, and Phenomenology of Dirac Fermionic Dark Matter Effective Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11914)
*Subhajit Kala,Lipika Kolay,Soumitra Nandi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了费米子暗物质与标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）耦合的完整重整化群框架，推导了包含暗物质场的维度五和六算符的反常维数矩阵，并结合跑动与阈值匹配，将高能新物理与多类低能实验观测量联系起来；通过电弱精密测量、味物理、轻子味破坏衰变等多渠道数据，对暗物质有效场论（DMEFT）的威尔逊系数给出严格且互补的限制，间接探测新物理能标可达数十至数百TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统研究费米子暗物质与标准模型之间的间接相互作用，需建立一个自洽、可跑动的有效场论框架，以连接高能新物理与低能实验约束。

Method: 构建包含费米子暗物质场的扩展SMEFT（即DMEFT），推导所有相关维度五和六算符的反常维数矩阵，结合重整化群方程跑动与阈值匹配，实现跨能标的威尔逊系数演化；进而计算各类可观测量（电弱精度、味物理、LFV、FCNC、不可见介子衰变等）并拟合当前实验数据。

Result: 获得了对DMEFT威尔逊系数的一系列严格且互补的实验约束；对应的新物理能标限制覆盖TeV至数百TeV量级，表明间接探测对暗区相互作用具有极高灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该工作为费米子暗物质的间接探测提供了首个完整的、可跑动的有效场论分析框架，显著提升了从低能精密测量中提取高能新物理信息的能力。

Abstract: We explore a fermionic dark matter (DM) extension of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) and establish a complete renormalisation-group framework for its phenomenological investigation. We derive the anomalous-dimension matrix of all relevant dimension-five and dimension-six operators involving Standard Model (SM) and DM fields, enabling the consistent evolution of the associated Wilson coefficients (WCs) across energy scales. By combining renormalisation-group running with matching at the relevant thresholds, we construct a robust bridge between high-scale new physics and experimental observables. We then perform a comprehensive phenomenological analysis, evaluating the contributions of these operators to observables spanning a wide range of energies and deriving constraints on the WCs from current data. We obtain stringent and complementary bounds on the DM effective field theory (DMEFT) WCs from electroweak precision observables, flavour processes, lepton-flavour-violating decays, top-quark flavour-changing neutral-current decays, and invisible meson decays. Interpreted in terms of the effective scale of new physics, the resulting limits demonstrate that current precision measurements probe energy scales ranging from the TeV regime to several tens or even hundreds of TeV, highlighting the remarkable sensitivity of indirect searches to dark-sector interactions.

</details>

### [135] [Properties of the positive and negative parity charm-strange and bottom-strange mesons $D_s$, $D_s^*$, $D_{s0}^*$, $D_{s1}$, $B_s$, $B_s^*$, $B_{s0}^*$, $B_{s1}$ from lattice QCD: masses, decay constants, and compositeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11026)
*Forrest Guyton,Stefan Meinel*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用格点QCD方法，结合RBC/UKQCD的七组不同参数的格点组态，系统计算了最轻的重-奇介子（标量、赝标量、矢量、轴矢量）的衰变常数、束缚能及Weinberg复合性参数，首次给出了B_s^*0和B_s1介子的衰变常数，并通过Lüscher方法外推得到无穷体积质量，结果支持正宇称态以分子态为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定重-奇介子（尤其是正宇称激发态）的基本性质，检验其内部结构（如四夸克态或分子态），并为强子物理模型提供关键格点QCD输入。

Method: 采用域壁费米子描述轻夸克与奇异夸克，各向异性clover作用量描述粲夸克与底夸克；使用七组RBC/UKQCD格点组态（π质量139–431 MeV，格距0.073–0.114 fm），联合进行手征与连续极限外推；对负宇称态直接提取衰变常数；对正宇称态采用GEVP方法分析含协变导数和介子-介子散射算符的相关矩阵，并用Lüscher方法获取无穷体积束缚态质量及Weinberg复合性参数。

Result: 获得D_s、D_s^*、B_s、B_s^*等负宇称介子高精度衰变常数；首次给出B_s^*0与B_s1的衰变常数（f_{B^*_{s0}}=207(12)(8)(2) MeV，f_{B_{s1}}=196(16)(11)(2) MeV）；得到正宇称介子相对于D/B+K阈值的负束缚能（如m_{B_{s1}}−m_{B^*}−m_K = −77(10)(5) MeV），且Weinberg参数支持其分子态主导结构。

Conclusion: 格点QCD计算表明，正宇称重-奇介子（D_s0^*, D_s1, B_s0^*, B_s1）具有显著负束缚能，Weinberg复合性分析一致支持其为介子-介子分子态主导的结构，而非紧致四夸克态；该结果为理解XYZ粒子谱及强相互作用非微扰机制提供了坚实依据。

Abstract: We present a lattice-QCD determination of properties of the lightest scalar, pseudoscalar, vector, and axial-vector heavy-strange mesons. This includes the decay constants of all mesons, and the binding energies and Weinberg compositeness parameters of the positive-parity states. The calculations are performed with domain-wall fermions for the light and strange quarks and anisotropic clover actions for the charm and bottom quarks. We use seven ensembles generated by RBC/UKQCD with pion masses ranging from 431 MeV to 139 MeV and lattice spacings ranging from 0.114 fm to 0.073 fm, which allows us to perform combined chiral and continuum extrapolations. For the negative-parity mesons, we obtain $f_{D_s}=251.4(2.1)(0.4)(2.5)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{D_s^*}=272.5(4.3)(1.0)(2.7)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{B_s}=228.4(5.8)(0.5)(2.3)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{B_s^*}=229.2(4.4)(0.8)(2.3)\:{\rm MeV}$, $f_{D_s^*}/f_{D_s}=1.086(14)(11)$, and $f_{B_s^*}/f_{B_s}=1.003(22)(10)$. In the positive-parity sector, the finite-volume energies and decay constants are extracted using the GEVP from correlation matrices with three different types of hadron interpolating operators, including operators with covariant derivatives and meson-meson-scattering operators at both source and sink. After extrapolation to the physical point, we obtain $f_{D^*_{s0}}=136.6 (8.0)(4.0)(1.4)$ MeV, $f_{D_{s1}}=200 (33)(24)(2)$ MeV, $f_{B^*_{s0}}=207 (12)(8)(2)$ MeV, and $f_{B_{s1}}= 196 (16)(11)(2)$ MeV. Our results for $f_{B^*_{s0}}$ and $f_{B_{s1}}$ are the first from lattice QCD. Lüscher's method is used to find the infinite-volume bound-state masses. At the physical point, we obtain $m_{D^*_{s0}}-m_D-m_K=-48 (14)(4)$ MeV, $m_{D_{s1}}-m_{D^*}-m_K=-61 (15)(2)$ MeV, $m_{B^*_{s0}}-m_B-m_K= -69 (13)(4)$ MeV, and $m_{B_{s1}}-m_{B^*}-m_K=-77 (10)(5)$ MeV. Our analysis shows consistency with the positive-parity states being predominantly molecular.

</details>
